      - paragraph [ref=e20560]: "Молодий посильний-коридорний Тед змушений працювати на зміні сам, та ще й напередодні Нового року. Цю свою вахту він запам’ятає на все життя, адже за одну зміну Тед пережив стільки, що декому не пережити і за одне життя: він потрапляє на шабаш відьом, ледве не гине від рук ревнивого чоловіка, ледве виживає від пустощів діточок гангстера та бере участь в дивному парі ексцентричних селебрітіз. Втім, до честі Теда, незважаючи на всі сумнівні пригоди, йому вдається зберегти незворушність в будь-яких ситуаціях…"

  107 | });
  108 | Then('every ceremony header is a curated English name', async ({ ctx, page }) => {
  109 |   const modal = modalOf(ctx, page);
  110 |   const re = pillPattern(await curatedNames(page));
  111 |   const n = await modal.ceremonyNames().count();
  112 |   expect(n).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  113 |   for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
  114 |     const text = (await modal.ceremonyNames().nth(i).innerText()).trim();
  115 |     expect(text, `ceremony "${text}" is not a curated English name`).toMatch(re);
  116 |     expect(text, `ceremony "${text}" contains Cyrillic — award names stay English (A-3)`)
  117 |       .not.toMatch(/[\u0400-\u04FF]/);
  118 |   }
  119 | });
  120 | Then('every category bullet reads in Ukrainian', async ({ ctx, page }) => {
  121 |   const modal = modalOf(ctx, page);

  143 | Then('the popover disappears', async ({ ctx, page }) => {

  147 | /* ---- scroll lock ---- */
  148 | Then('background scroll is locked via position fixed', async ({ catalog, page }) => {
  149 |   expect(await catalog.bodyIsScrollLocked()).toBe(true);
  150 |   expect(await page.evaluate(() => getComputedStyle(document.body).position)).toBe('fixed');
  151 | });
  152 | Then('background scroll is locked', async ({ catalog }) => {
  153 |   expect(await catalog.bodyIsScrollLocked()).toBe(true);
  154 | });
  155 | Then('background scroll is unlocked', async ({ catalog }) => {
  156 |   expect(await catalog.bodyIsScrollLocked()).toBe(false);
  157 | });
  158 | Then('scroll is unlocked and the position is restored', async ({ catalog, ctx }) => {
  159 |   expect(await catalog.bodyIsScrollLocked()).toBe(false);
  160 |   // scroll-behavior: smooth ANIMATES the restoring scrollTo — poll until it
  161 |   // lands; ±2px absorbs mobile-emulation rounding.
  162 |   await expect.poll(async () => Math.abs(await catalog.scrollY() - ctx.scrollBefore))
  163 |     .toBeLessThanOrEqual(2);
  164 | });
  165 |

  167 | When('I open the add modal', async ({ ctx, page }) => {

  171 | When('I close the add modal', async ({ ctx }) => {

  180 | /* ---- "Де подивитись" ----
  181 |  * Read-only, and deliberately never follows a provider link: part of what
  182 |  * these assert is that the app cannot spend money, and a CI run clicking
  183 |  * through to a storefront would be a poor way to make that argument. */
  184 | 
  185 | // The offer kinds the app can render, cheapest-for-the-viewer first. Mirrors
  186 | // PROVIDER_KIND_ORDER in src/logic.js; kept here rather than read off the page
  187 | // because the ORDER is the assertion — reading it from the thing under test
  188 | // would make the scenario agree with any order it happened to produce.
  189 | const KIND_ORDER = ['Передплата', 'Безкоштовно', 'Безкоштовно з рекламою', 'Є в каталозі', 'Оренда', 'Купівля'];
  190 | 
  191 | Given('a movie modal with providers is open', async ({ catalog, ctx, page }) => {
  192 |   const i = await catalog.firstCardIndexWithProviders();
  193 |   // Not a failure: no film has providers until the backfill has run, and a
  194 |   // red smoke suite rolls the live site back a release.
  195 |   test.skip(i === -1, 'no film in the catalog has providers on file yet');
  196 |   await catalog.openCard(i);
  197 |   await modalOf(ctx, page).waitUntilOpen();
  198 |   ctx.providers = await modalOf(ctx, page).providers();
  199 |   expect(ctx.providers.length, 'the film has providers on file but rendered no rows')
  200 |     .toBeGreaterThan(0);
  201 | });
  202 | 
  203 | Then('every provider row names a service and what the offer is', async ({ ctx }) => {
  204 |   for (const p of ctx.providers) {
  205 |     expect(p.name, 'a provider row rendered with no name').toBeTruthy();
  206 |     expect(KIND_ORDER, `"${p.name}" shows an offer label the app does not define: "${p.kind}"`)
> 207 |       .toContain(p.kind);
      |        ^ Error: "Megogo" shows an offer label the app does not define: ""
  208 |   }
  209 | });
  210 | 
  211 | Then("every provider row carries the service's own logo", async ({ ctx }) => {
  212 |   for (const p of ctx.providers) {
  213 |     expect(p.hasLogo, `"${p.name}" rendered without a logo or its fallback initial`).toBe(true);
  214 |   }
  215 | });
  216 | 
  217 | Then('providers are ordered from subscription to purchase', async ({ ctx }) => {
  218 |   const ranks = ctx.providers.map(p => KIND_ORDER.indexOf(p.kind));
  219 |   const sorted = [...ranks].sort((a, b) => a - b);
  220 |   expect(ranks, `rows are out of cost order: ${ctx.providers.map(p => `${p.name}/${p.kind}`).join(', ')}`)
  221 |     .toEqual(sorted);
  222 | });
  223 | 
  224 | Then('every provider link opens in a new tab with rel="noopener"', async ({ ctx }) => {
  225 |   const links = ctx.providers.filter(p => p.href);
  226 |   expect(links.length, 'not one provider row was a link').toBeGreaterThan(0);
  227 |   for (const p of links) {
  228 |     expect(p.newTab, `"${p.name}" would navigate away from the app`).toBe(true);
  229 |     expect(p.rel, `"${p.name}" opens a new tab without rel="noopener"`).toContain('noopener');
  230 |   }
  231 | });
  232 | 
  233 | Then('every provider link points at a film page, never a checkout', async ({ ctx }) => {

  236 |       .not.toMatch(/checkout|\/buy\b|payment|purchase|subscribe/i);
